## Runbooks
*Every simmed archetype, most prevalent first (the field guide reads these; the PDF keeps full runbooks for the top few + compact cards for the rest).*

### Adeptus Custodes — Shield Host  ·  11 in field  ·  FAVOURABLE — out-OC + grind

**How to play:** Elite-but-FEW (W3-4, ~40 models). Out-OC (your OC10 vs their OC2) and grind their bodies with anti-infantry VOLUME (Avenger/RFBC/plasma). Volcano/thermal the Caladius + characters, NOT W3 Custodians (overkill). Lancer SWEEP (A10 S10) chews squads; target-rich for decapitation. Don't get greedy into their melee.

```
RUNBOOK — This List Tastes Like Death by Rock and Roll  vs  Adrian Perez — Adeptus Custodes / Shield Host
  you play Destroyer's Wrath  |  they play Vital Link  |  deployment: crucible  (120 games simulated)

READ: FAVOURED — you hold the board and out-last them; the game grinds your way.
WIN CONDITION: Grind: trade your durable bodies into theirs and sit on the objectives. Time is on your side.
POSTURE: play grind — advance and trade; you win the attrition on the objectives.
DEPLOYMENT: deploy centrally; push your durable bodies onto the mid-board objectives and trade forward.

BOARD CONTROL you vs them (avg objectives, R1->R5):
   you : 0.6 1.1 0.8 1.0 0.9
   them: 1.4 1.3 0.3 0.2 0.1

BOARD LYNCHPINS — who HOLDS the objectives  (ANCHOR = durable, contest/out-position · SOFT = chaff, clear with volume):
   - Custodian Guard                OC  40  5u @OC2  ANCHOR · hurts you
   - Allarus Custodians             OC  36  3u @OC2  ANCHOR · hurts you
   - Witchseekers                   OC  10  2u @OC1  SOFT   · hurts you
   (OC N = board total; '5u @OC2' = 5 units at OC2 per model; buffs/sticky in the how-to-play note)

PRIORITY KILLS (they hurt you AND you can remove them — kill in this order):
   1. 5x Custodian Guard             deals 21.0 w/turn to you, you remove it 92% of games
   2. 3x Allarus Custodians          deals 18.1 w/turn to you, you remove it 99% of games
   3. Shield-Captain in Allarus Terminator Armour deals 6.6 w/turn to you, you remove it 98% of games
   4. 2x Witchseekers                deals 3.3 w/turn to you, you remove it 63% of games

PLAY AROUND (you CAN'T reliably remove these — screen / avoid / out-score, don't feed them):
   (none — you can remove their key pieces)

YOUR WORKHORSES (do the damage — leverage + protect these):
   - Cerastus Knight Lancer         30.3 w/turn, survives 1%
   - 2x Knight Castellan            11.1 w/turn, survives 78%
   - Knight Crusader                6.5 w/turn, survives 3%
   - Armiger Warglaive              2.3 w/turn, survives 0%

YOUR LIABILITIES (die before earning their points — protect, hold back, or reconsider):
   - Armiger Warglaive              survives only 0%
   - Navigator                      survives only 4%

SECONDARIES that pay here (score these when the matchup hands them to you):
   + No Prisoners
   + Overwhelming Force
   + Assassination (you can reach their characters)
   + Display of Might
   + Centre Ground

KEY STRATAGEMS this matchup: lean on your Valourstrike Lance defensive strats early, save CP for Counter-Offensive on their key charge.

THE TRAP: exposing Armiger Warglaive — it dies for nothing. Screen it or hold it back until it matters.
```

### Imperial Knights — Valourstrike Lance  ·  11 in field  ·  MIRROR — the ditto

**How to play:** Knightball mirror (your own build). First turn + Rotate discipline decides it. Trade Volcano/plasma into their Questoris, Lancer hunts their Lancer/Castellan (Epic Challenge to snipe an attached char), win the secondary game. Don't lose a gun-Knight to their Lancer un-Rotated.

```
RUNBOOK — This List Tastes Like Death by Rock and Roll  vs  Alex Hernandez — Imperial Knights / Valourstrike Lance
  you play Meatgrinder  |  they play Meatgrinder  |  deployment: short-edge  (120 games simulated)

READ: FAVOURED — you hold the board and out-last them; the game grinds your way.
WIN CONDITION: Grind: trade your durable bodies into theirs and sit on the objectives. Time is on your side.
POSTURE: play brace — meet the alpha from cover; counter-punch after their charge lands.
DEPLOYMENT: deploy BACK, in/behind cover near your primary — deny a turn-1 alpha and make them come to you; screen your home objectives (they'll try to get behind you).

   them: 0.6 0.3 0.1 0.1 0.1
```

### Space Marines (Astartes) — Librarius Conclave  ·  10 in field  ·  COIN-FLIP → hard (kill the engine)

**How to play:** The Great Value engine: Dev-Wound Sternguard + Librarian Conclave discipline + Oath re-rolls stack a volley that IGNORES your invuln. KILL THE MULTIPLIER first (Librarian / re-roll or mark engine / Storm Speeder) — the naked bricks are chaff without it. Screen the alpha; don't cluster near a reactive shooter.

```
RUNBOOK — This List Tastes Like Death by Rock and Roll  vs  Aaron Rojas — Space Marines (Astartes) / Librarius Conclave
  you play Destroyer's Wrath  |  they play Vital Link  |  deployment: crucible  (120 games simulated)

READ: HARD — you get OUT-CONTROLLED: they out-body you and hold more of the board where it counts.
WIN CONDITION: You can't out-body them — you must out-KILL to thin their scoring + deny objectives, or you lose the primary. Prioritise clearing the units ON the points, not the ones threatening you.
POSTURE: play grind — advance and trade; you win the attrition on the objectives.
DEPLOYMENT: deploy centrally; push your durable bodies onto the mid-board objectives and trade forward; screen your home objectives (they'll try to get behind you).
```

### Necrons — Cursed Legion  ·  10 in field  ·  UNFAVOURABLE (~20-30%)

**How to play:** 54 Cold-Fervour Gauss (S7 AP-2 Lethal) + reanimation + out-OC, often an un-killable Void Dragon. Firepower-as-DENIAL: delete their soft OC to starve scoring, don't chase the un-killable. Accept the board loss, bank mobility secondaries, win on tempo not attrition.

